## Setup and notation

- D = unit ball in R^d, d ≥ 2·: f ∈ C^α(D) (Hölder-Zygmund, α > 1), f = 1 + h, ‖h‖_{C^α} ≤ r·: u_f: −div(f∇u) = 1 (sign: as given: div(f∇u) = 1, u|∂D = 0)·: u_1 = (|x|²−1)/(2d)·:
- White noise model: ⟨Y, φ⟩ = ⟨u_f, φ⟩ + n^{−1/2}⟨W, φ⟩·:
- Class: F(α,r)·:
- Goal: Theorem: for fixed r ∈ (0, r_0(α,d)) small: minimax MSE ≍ n^{−2α/(2α+d+3)} up to log factors·:

c·r^{2(d+3)/(2α+d+3)}·n^{−2α/(2α+d+3)} ≤ inf-sup ≤ C·(log n)^{c'}·r^{2(d+3)/(2α+d+3)}·n^{−2α/(2α+d+3)}
hence the minimax rate is n^{−2α/(2α+d+3)} up to logarithmic factors·: (MSE·: L²-risk rate n^{−α/(2α+d+3)}·)
